## Scenario runs

The change bench (vision 27, ADR-033): `cmd/scenariobench` over a corpus of
scenarios (`testdata/scenariobench/*.json`) — a change to a pinned subject,
run by an agent across sessions under a turn budget, in arms that differ
only in data, scored by oracles fathom does not own
(`internal/scenariobench`, `docs/plans/strengths-bench.md`). Each row below
is one (run, task, arm); the full per-cell data lives in
`docs/bench/scenarios-ledger.jsonl`, appended by `scenariobench ledger` and
never hand-edited.

<!-- BEGIN scenario ledger (task bench-scenario-ledger) -->

### Where fathom stands

**findings-loop-r4** — full vs control: success 2/15 vs 3/15, tokens 1.109×, cost 1.061×.
Surface calls: control=35, full=28.
On findings-loop-r4, fathom's arm lost to control on success (2 v 3) at 1.109× the tokens; the tools were called 28 time(s). Registered criterion FL-24-1: fail (treatment success rate 0.133 is below control's 0.200).
gold source: mcp__fathom__find_symbol earned 2 of 2 passes.
Paired (bootstrap, n=5, seed 20260902, 20000 resamples): success-rate diff -0.067 [-0.200, +0.000] (95% CI); token ratio 1.17× [0.92×, 1.41×].
